Acknowledgements
Dear Customer, 
Schnell would like to thank you for choosing one of its numerous products and inform you about the issue of this booklet which will help you appreciate 
the quality of its Spare Parts Department.
We recommend you to attentively consult it to easily locate the components which will have to be replaced; this will facilitate the placing of your next 
order.
Introduction
In this spare parts booklet you can find instructions which will help you, step by step, in the individuation of the machine data and its units, in the 
discovery of the single components detectable in the exploded tables and the relating legends.         
In those tables you can find the machine in its whole, its principal units and single details exclusively concerning the mechanical part. With regard to 
the oleodynamic electrical and pneumatic units and components, we suggest you to consult the Directory of Schemes and Diagrams supplied with the 
machine.
Upon reception of the goods, the non-relationship of the article code could be verified between the documents and the spare parts booklet because 
Schnell takes care to send always the last technical updating of the spare part, respecting its interchangeability.  
The enclosed order form has been conceived to better help you ordering the necessary components; we  kindly ask you to make a copy of it for your 
future order and to fill in the form in all its parts. This will allow us to grant you our best quality service, shipping the proper spare part as soon as pos-
sible.   
The guarantee conditions applied are the same ones as those of the Information for Use Manual supplied with the machine.
For greater safety and long use of the machine, please use only SCHNELL spare parts.
We thank you for your continuous and precious collaboration and welcome any suggestion
you may have that will help us improve our products and services.
Best Regards
THE MANAGEMENT
SCHNELL GROUP
Acknowledgements and introduction

How to place a spare parts order
The enclosed spare parts order form will help you appreciate the quality of our Spare Parts Department. We kindly ask you to make a copy of it for your 
future order and to fill in the form in all its parts (picture C), including data about the machine identification and components. Sp we will grant you our 
best quality service, shipping you the proper required spare part as soon as possible.
In order to correctly individuate your machine, please transmit us the following data well shown in the identification plate: the machine model and the 
serial numer (picture A). To exactly identify your component, please give us precise information detectable in the spare parts booklet: the unit code 
number, the position number, the component code number, the quantity and the relating description (picture B).
Example:
SPARE PARTS DEPT.
